CORE-1>ena
CORE-1#

Enter configuration commands, one per line.  End with CNTL/Z.

CORE-1(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

CORE-1(config-if)#
%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/1, changed state to down

%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/1, changed state to up

CORE-1(config-if)#exit
CORE-1(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/2
CORE-1(config-if)#switchport trunk encapsulation dot1q
CORE-1(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

CORE-1(config-if)#
%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/2, changed state to down

%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/2, changed state to up

CORE-1(config-if)#exit

CORE-2(config-if)#

%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/1, changed state to up

CORE-2(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/2

CORE-2(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

Conectar equipos a su respectiva VLAN

SW-Piso2(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso2(config-if)#switchport access vlan 53


SW-Piso3(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso3(config-if)#switchport access vlan 53


SW-Piso4(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso4(config-if)#switchport access vlan 52
_____________________________________

Configurar los Gateway en los SW Core

CORE-1(config)#interface vlan 52
CORE-1(config-if)#ip address 192.168.117.1 255.255.255.0
CORE-1(config-if)#exit
CORE-1(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-1(config-if)#ip address 172.16.117.1 255.255.255.0



CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 52
CORE-2(config-if)#ip address 192.168.117.1 255.255.255.0
CORE-2(config-if)#
CORE-2(config-if)#exit
CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-2(config-if)#ip address 172.16.117.1 255.255.255.0

___________________________________

Finalmente, configurar el respectivo Gateway en los pc según la vlan a la que pertenece

___________________________________

Dar prioridad

CORE-1(config)#interface vlan 52
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 52 ip 192.168.117.1
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 52 priority 110


CORE-1(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 53 ip 172.16.117.1
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 53 priority 110

_____________________________________

CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 52
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 52 ip 192.168.117.1
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 52 priority 100

CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 53 ip 172.16.117.1
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 53 priority 100


_____________________________________

interface gigabitethernet0/1
switchport mode trunk
switchport trunk allowed vlan 52,53

Hacer lo mismo para fastEthernet 0/2 en los core

CORE-2(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport mode trunk
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport trunk allowed vlan 52,53

CORE-2(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

CORE-2(config-if)#
%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/1, changed state to down

%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/1, changed state to up

CORE-2(config-if)#exit
CORE-2(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/2
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport trunk encapsulation dot1q
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport mode trunk



_____________________________________

Configurar interfaces troncales en los SW Core

SW-Piso1(config)#interface gigabitEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso1(config-if)#switchport mode trunk


SW-Piso2(config)#interface gigabitEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso2(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

SW-Piso3(config)#interface gigabitEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso3(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

__________________________________

Conectar equipos a su respectiva VLAN

SW-Piso1(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso1(config-if)#switchport access vlan 52


SW-Piso2(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso2(config-if)#switchport access vlan 53


SW-Piso3(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso3(config-if)#switchport access vlan 53


SW-Piso4(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso4(config-if)#switchport access vlan 52
_____________________________________

Configurar los Gateway en los SW Core

CORE-1(config)#interface vlan 52
CORE-1(config-if)#ip address 192.168.117.1 255.255.255.0
CORE-1(config-if)#exit
CORE-1(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-1(config-if)#ip address 172.16.117.1 255.255.255.0



CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 52
CORE-2(config-if)#ip address 192.168.117.1 255.255.255.0
CORE-2(config-if)#
CORE-2(config-if)#exit
CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-2(config-if)#ip address 172.16.117.1 255.255.255.0

___________________________________

Finalmente, configurar el respectivo Gateway en los pc según la vlan a la que pertenece

___________________________________

Dar prioridad

CORE-1(config)#interface vlan 52
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 52 ip 192.168.117.1
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 52 priority 110


CORE-1(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 53 ip 172.16.117.1
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 53 priority 110

_____________________________________

CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 52
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 52 ip 192.168.117.1
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 52 priority 100

CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 53 ip 172.16.117.1
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 53 priority 100


_____________________________________

interface gigabitethernet0/1
switchport mode trunk
switchport trunk allowed vlan 52,53

Hacer lo mismo para fastEthernet 0/2 en los core

Configurar interfaces troncales en los SW Core

CORE-1>ena
CORE-1#
CORE-1#conf ter
Enter configuration commands, one per line.  End with CNTL/Z.
CORE-1(config)#
CORE-1(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
CORE-1(config-if)#switchport trunk encapsulation dot1q
CORE-1(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

CORE-1(config-if)#
%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/1, changed state to down

%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/1, changed state to up

CORE-1(config-if)#exit
CORE-1(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/2
CORE-1(config-if)#switchport trunk encapsulation dot1q
CORE-1(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

CORE-1(config-if)#
%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/2, changed state to down

%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/2, changed state to up

CORE-1(config-if)#exit

______________________________________

CORE-2>ena
CORE-2#
CORE-2#conf ter
Enter configuration commands, one per line.  End with CNTL/Z.
CORE-2(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
CORE-2(config-if)#
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport trunk encapsulation dot1q
CORE-2(config-if)#
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

CORE-2(config-if)#
%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/1, changed state to down

%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/1, changed state to up

CORE-2(config-if)#exit
CORE-2(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/2
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport trunk encapsulation dot1q
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport mode trunk



_____________________________________

Configurar interfaces troncales en los SW Core

SW-Piso1(config)#interface gigabitEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso1(config-if)#switchport mode trunk


SW-Piso2(config)#interface gigabitEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso2(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

SW-Piso3(config)#interface gigabitEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso3(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

__________________________________

Conectar equipos a su respectiva VLAN

SW-Piso1(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso1(config-if)#switchport access vlan 52


SW-Piso2(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso2(config-if)#switchport access vlan 53


SW-Piso3(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso3(config-if)#switchport access vlan 53


SW-Piso4(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso4(config-if)#switchport access vlan 52
_____________________________________

Configurar los Gateway en los SW Core

CORE-1(config)#interface vlan 52
CORE-1(config-if)#ip address 192.168.117.1 255.255.255.0
CORE-1(config-if)#exit
CORE-1(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-1(config-if)#ip address 172.16.117.1 255.255.255.0



CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 52
CORE-2(config-if)#ip address 192.168.117.1 255.255.255.0
CORE-2(config-if)#
CORE-2(config-if)#exit
CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-2(config-if)#ip address 172.16.117.1 255.255.255.0

___________________________________

Finalmente, configurar el respectivo Gateway en los pc según la vlan a la que pertenece

___________________________________

Dar prioridad

CORE-1(config)#interface vlan 52
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 52 ip 192.168.117.1
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 52 priority 110


CORE-1(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 53 ip 172.16.117.1
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 53 priority 110

_____________________________________

CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 52
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 52 ip 192.168.117.1
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 52 priority 100

CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 53 ip 172.16.117.1
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 53 priority 100


_____________________________________

interface gigabitethernet0/1
switchport mode trunk
switchport trunk allowed vlan 52,53

Hacer lo mismo para fastEthernet 0/2 en los core

CORE-2(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport mode trunk
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport trunk allowed vlan 52,53

Configurar interfaces troncales en los SW Core

CORE-1>ena
CORE-1#
CORE-1#conf ter
Enter configuration commands, one per line.  End with CNTL/Z.
CORE-1(config)#
CORE-1(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
CORE-1(config-if)#switchport trunk encapsulation dot1q
CORE-1(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

CORE-1(config-if)#
%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/1, changed state to down

%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/1, changed state to up

CORE-1(config-if)#exit
CORE-1(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/2
CORE-1(config-if)#switchport trunk encapsulation dot1q
CORE-1(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

CORE-1(config-if)#
%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/2, changed state to down

%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/2, changed state to up

CORE-1(config-if)#exit

CORE-2(config-if)#
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

CORE-2(config-if)#
%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/1, changed state to down

CORE-2(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/2

CORE-2(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

Configurar interfaces troncales en los SW Core

Conectar equipos a su respectiva VLAN

SW-Piso1(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso1(config-if)#switchport access vlan 52


SW-Piso2(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso2(config-if)#switchport access vlan 53


SW-Piso3(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso3(config-if)#switchport access vlan 53


SW-Piso4(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso4(config-if)#switchport access vlan 52
_____________________________________

Configurar los Gateway en los SW Core

CORE-1(config)#interface vlan 52
CORE-1(config-if)#ip address 192.168.117.1 255.255.255.0
CORE-1(config-if)#exit
CORE-1(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-1(config-if)#ip address 172.16.117.1 255.255.255.0



CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 52
CORE-2(config-if)#ip address 192.168.117.1 255.255.255.0
CORE-2(config-if)#
CORE-2(config-if)#exit
CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-2(config-if)#ip address 172.16.117.1 255.255.255.0

___________________________________

Finalmente, configurar el respectivo Gateway en los pc según la vlan a la que pertenece

___________________________________

Dar prioridad

CORE-1(config)#interface vlan 52
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 52 ip 192.168.117.1
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 52 priority 110


CORE-1(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 53 ip 172.16.117.1
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 53 priority 110

_____________________________________

CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 52
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 52 ip 192.168.117.1
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 52 priority 100

CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 53 ip 172.16.117.1
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 53 priority 100


_____________________________________

interface gigabitethernet0/1
switchport mode trunk
switchport trunk allowed vlan 52,53

Hacer lo mismo para fastEthernet 0/2 en los core

CORE-2(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport mode trunk
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport trunk allowed vlan 52,53

CORE-1(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-1(config-if)#ip address 172.16.117.1 255.255.255.0



CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 52
CORE-2(config-if)#ip address 192.168.117.1 255.255.255.0
CORE-2(config-if)#
CORE-2(config-if)#exit
CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-2(config-if)#ip address 172.16.117.1 255.255.255.0

___________________________________

Finalmente, configurar el respectivo Gateway en los pc según la vlan a la que pertenece

___________________________________

Dar prioridad

CORE-1(config)#interface vlan 52
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 52 ip 192.168.117.1
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 52 priority 110


CORE-1(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 53 ip 172.16.117.1
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 53 priority 110

_____________________________________

CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 52
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 52 ip 192.168.117.1
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 52 priority 100

CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 53 ip 172.16.117.1
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 53 priority 100


_____________________________________

interface gigabitethernet0/1
switchport mode trunk
switchport trunk allowed vlan 52,53

Hacer lo mismo para fastEthernet 0/2 en los core

CORE-2(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport mode trunk
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport trunk allowed vlan 52,53

Configurar interfaces troncales en los SW Core

CORE-1>ena
CORE-1#
CORE-1#conf ter
Enter configuration commands, one per line.  End with CNTL/Z.
CORE-1(config)#
CORE-1(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
CORE-1(config-if)#switchport trunk encapsulation dot1q
CORE-1(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

CORE-1(config-if)#
%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/1, changed state to down

%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/1, changed state to up

CORE-1(config-if)#exit
CORE-1(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/2
CORE-1(config-if)#switchport trunk encapsulation dot1q
CORE-1(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

CORE-1(config-if)#
%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/2, changed state to down

%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/2, changed state to up

CORE-1(config-if)#exit

______________________________________

%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/1, changed state to up

CORE-2(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

Configurar interfaces troncales en los SW Core

CORE-1>ena
CORE-1#
CORE-1#conf ter
Enter configuration commands, one per line.  End with CNTL/Z.
CORE-1(config)#
CORE-1(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
CORE-1(config-if)#switchport trunk encapsulation dot1q
CORE-1(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

CORE-1(config-if)#
%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/1, changed state to down

%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/1, changed state to up

CORE-1(config-if)#exit
CORE-1(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/2
CORE-1(config-if)#switchport trunk encapsulation dot1q
CORE-1(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

CORE-1(config-if)#
%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/2, changed state to down

%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/2, changed state to up

CORE-1(config-if)#exit

______________________________________

CORE-2>ena
CORE-2#
CORE-2#conf ter
Enter configuration commands, one per line.  End with CNTL/Z.
CORE-2(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
CORE-2(config-if)#
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport trunk encapsulation dot1q
CORE-2(config-if)#
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

CORE-2(config-if)#
%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/1, changed state to down

%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/1, changed state to up

CORE-2(config-if)#exit
CORE-2(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/2
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport trunk encapsulation dot1q
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport mode trunk



_____________________________________

Configurar interfaces troncales en los SW Core

SW-Piso1(config)#interface gigabitEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso1(config-if)#switchport mode trunk


SW-Piso2(config)#interface gigabitEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso2(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

SW-Piso3(config)#interface gigabitEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso3(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

__________________________________

Conectar equipos a su respectiva VLAN

SW-Piso1(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso1(config-if)#switchport access vlan 52


SW-Piso2(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso2(config-if)#switchport access vlan 53


SW-Piso3(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso3(config-if)#switchport access vlan 53


SW-Piso4(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso4(config-if)#switchport access vlan 52
_____________________________________

Configurar los Gateway en los SW Core

CORE-1(config)#interface vlan 52
CORE-1(config-if)#ip address 192.168.117.1 255.255.255.0
CORE-1(config-if)#exit
CORE-1(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-1(config-if)#ip address 172.16.117.1 255.255.255.0



CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 52
CORE-2(config-if)#ip address 192.168.117.1 255.255.255.0
CORE-2(config-if)#
CORE-2(config-if)#exit
CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-2(config-if)#ip address 172.16.117.1 255.255.255.0

___________________________________

Finalmente, configurar el respectivo Gateway en los pc según la vlan a la que pertenece

___________________________________

Dar prioridad

CORE-1(config)#interface vlan 52
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 52 ip 192.168.117.1
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 52 priority 110


CORE-1(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 53 ip 172.16.117.1
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 53 priority 110

_____________________________________

CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 52
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 52 ip 192.168.117.1
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 52 priority 100

CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 53 ip 172.16.117.1
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 53 priority 100


_____________________________________

interface gigabitethernet0/1
switchport mode trunk
switchport trunk allowed vlan 52,53

Hacer lo mismo para fastEthernet 0/2 en los core

CORE-2(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport mode trunk
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport trunk allowed vlan 52,53

%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/1, changed state to down

%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/1, changed state to up

CORE-1(config-if)#exit
CORE-1(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/2
CORE-1(config-if)#switchport trunk encapsulation dot1q
CORE-1(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

CORE-1(config-if)#
%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/2, changed state to down

%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/2, changed state to up

CORE-1(config-if)#exit

______________________________________

CORE-2#

CORE-2(config-if)#

CORE-2(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

Configurar interfaces troncales en los SW Core

SW-Piso1(config-if)#switchport mode trunk


SW-Piso2(config)#interface gigabitEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso2(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

SW-Piso3(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

__________________________________

Conectar equipos a su respectiva VLAN

SW-Piso2(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso2(config-if)#switchport access vlan 53


SW-Piso3(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso3(config-if)#switchport access vlan 53


SW-Piso4(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso4(config-if)#switchport access vlan 52
_____________________________________

Configurar los Gateway en los SW Core

CORE-1(config)#interface vlan 52

CORE-1(config-if)#exit
CORE-1(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-1(config-if)#ip address 172.16.117.1 255.255.255.0



CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 52
CORE-2(config-if)#ip address 192.168.117.1 255.255.255.0
CORE-2(config-if)#
CORE-2(config-if)#exit
CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-2(config-if)#ip address 172.16.117.1 255.255.255.0

___________________________________

Finalmente, configurar el respectivo Gateway en los pc según la vlan a la que pertenece

___________________________________

Dar prioridad

CORE-1(config)#interface vlan 52
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 52 ip 192.168.117.1
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 52 priority 110


CORE-1(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 53 ip 172.16.117.1
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 53 priority 110

_____________________________________

CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 52
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 52 ip 192.168.117.1
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 52 priority 100

CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 53 ip 172.16.117.1
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 53 priority 100


_____________________________________

interface gigabitethernet0/1
switchport mode trunk
switchport trunk allowed vlan 52,53

Hacer lo mismo para fastEthernet 0/2 en los core

CORE-2(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport mode trunk
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport trunk allowed vlan 52,53

Configurar interfaces troncales en los SW Core

CORE-1>ena
CORE-1#
CORE-1#conf ter
Enter configuration commands, one per line.  End with CNTL/Z.
CORE-1(config)#
CORE-1(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
CORE-1(config-if)#switchport trunk encapsulation dot1q
CORE-1(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

CORE-1(config-if)#
%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/1, changed state to down

%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/1, changed state to up

CORE-1(config-if)#exit
CORE-1(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/2
CORE-1(config-if)#switchport trunk encapsulation dot1q
CORE-1(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

CORE-1(config-if)#
%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/2, changed state to down

%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/2, changed state to up

CORE-1(config-if)#exit

______________________________________

CORE-2>ena
CORE-2#
CORE-2#conf ter
Enter configuration commands, one per line.  End with CNTL/Z.

CORE-2(config-if)#
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport trunk encapsulation dot1q
CORE-2(config-if)#
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

CORE-2(config-if)#
%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/1, changed state to down

%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/1, changed state to up

CORE-2(config-if)#exit
CORE-2(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/2
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport trunk encapsulation dot1q
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport mode trunk



_____________________________________

Configurar interfaces troncales en los SW Core

SW-Piso1(config)#interface gigabitEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso1(config-if)#switchport mode trunk


SW-Piso2(config)#interface gigabitEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso2(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

SW-Piso3(config)#interface gigabitEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso3(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

Conectar equipos a su respectiva VLAN

SW-Piso2(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso2(config-if)#switchport access vlan 53


SW-Piso3(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso3(config-if)#switchport access vlan 53


SW-Piso4(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso4(config-if)#switchport access vlan 52

Configurar los Gateway en los SW Core

CORE-1(config)#interface vlan 52

CORE-1(config-if)#exit
CORE-1(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-1(config-if)#ip address 172.16.117.1 255.255.255.0



CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 52
CORE-2(config-if)#ip address 192.168.117.1 255.255.255.0
CORE-2(config-if)#
CORE-2(config-if)#exit
CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-2(config-if)#ip address 172.16.117.1 255.255.255.0

___________________________________

Finalmente, configurar el respectivo Gateway en los pc según la vlan a la que pertenece

___________________________________

Dar prioridad

CORE-1(config)#interface vlan 52
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 52 ip 192.168.117.1
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 52 priority 110


CORE-1(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 53 ip 172.16.117.1
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 53 priority 110

_____________________________________

CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 52
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 52 ip 192.168.117.1
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 52 priority 100

CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 53 ip 172.16.117.1
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 53 priority 100


_____________________________________

interface gigabitethernet0/1
switchport mode trunk
switchport trunk allowed vlan 52,53

Hacer lo mismo para fastEthernet 0/2 en los core

CORE-2(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport mode trunk
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport trunk allowed vlan 52,53

Configurar interfaces troncales en los SW Core

CORE-1>ena
CORE-1#
CORE-1#conf ter
Enter configuration commands, one per line.  End with CNTL/Z.
CORE-1(config)#
CORE-1(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
CORE-1(config-if)#switchport trunk encapsulation dot1q
CORE-1(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

CORE-1(config-if)#
%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/1, changed state to down

%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/1, changed state to up

CORE-1(config-if)#exit
CORE-1(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/2
CORE-1(config-if)#switchport trunk encapsulation dot1q
CORE-1(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

CORE-1(config-if)#
%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/2, changed state to down

%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/2, changed state to up

CORE-1(config-if)#exit

______________________________________

CORE-2>ena
CORE-2#

CORE-2(config-if)#
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport trunk encapsulation dot1q
CORE-2(config-if)#
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

CORE-2(config-if)#
%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/1, changed state to down

%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/1, changed state to up

CORE-2(config-if)#exit
CORE-2(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/2
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport trunk encapsulation dot1q
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

Configurar interfaces troncales en los SW Core

Conectar equipos a su respectiva VLAN

SW-Piso2(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso2(config-if)#switchport access vlan 53


SW-Piso3(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso3(config-if)#switchport access vlan 53


SW-Piso4(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso4(config-if)#switchport access vlan 52
_____________________________________

Configurar los Gateway en los SW Core

CORE-1(config)#interface vlan 52
CORE-1(config-if)#ip address 192.168.117.1 255.255.255.0
CORE-1(config-if)#exit
CORE-1(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-1(config-if)#ip address 172.16.117.1 255.255.255.0



CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 52
CORE-2(config-if)#ip address 192.168.117.1 255.255.255.0
CORE-2(config-if)#
CORE-2(config-if)#exit
CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-2(config-if)#ip address 172.16.117.1 255.255.255.0

___________________________________

Finalmente, configurar el respectivo Gateway en los pc según la vlan a la que pertenece

___________________________________

Dar prioridad

CORE-1(config)#interface vlan 52
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 52 ip 192.168.117.1
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 52 priority 110


CORE-1(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 53 ip 172.16.117.1
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 53 priority 110

_____________________________________

CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 52
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 52 ip 192.168.117.1
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 52 priority 100

CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 53 ip 172.16.117.1
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 53 priority 100


_____________________________________

interface gigabitethernet0/1
switchport mode trunk
switchport trunk allowed vlan 52,53

Hacer lo mismo para fastEthernet 0/2 en los core

CORE-2(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport mode trunk
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport trunk allowed vlan 52,53

SW-Piso2(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

SW-Piso3(config)#interface gigabitEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso3(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

__________________________________

Conectar equipos a su respectiva VLAN

SW-Piso1(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso1(config-if)#switchport access vlan 52


SW-Piso2(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso2(config-if)#switchport access vlan 53


SW-Piso3(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso3(config-if)#switchport access vlan 53


SW-Piso4(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso4(config-if)#switchport access vlan 52
_____________________________________

Configurar los Gateway en los SW Core

CORE-1(config)#interface vlan 52
CORE-1(config-if)#ip address 192.168.117.1 255.255.255.0
CORE-1(config-if)#exit
CORE-1(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-1(config-if)#ip address 172.16.117.1 255.255.255.0



CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 52
CORE-2(config-if)#ip address 192.168.117.1 255.255.255.0
CORE-2(config-if)#
CORE-2(config-if)#exit
CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-2(config-if)#ip address 172.16.117.1 255.255.255.0

___________________________________

Finalmente, configurar el respectivo Gateway en los pc según la vlan a la que pertenece

___________________________________

Dar prioridad

CORE-1(config)#interface vlan 52
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 52 ip 192.168.117.1
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 52 priority 110


CORE-1(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 53 ip 172.16.117.1
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 53 priority 110

_____________________________________

CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 52
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 52 ip 192.168.117.1
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 52 priority 100

CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 53 ip 172.16.117.1
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 53 priority 100


_____________________________________

interface gigabitethernet0/1
switchport mode trunk
switchport trunk allowed vlan 52,53

Hacer lo mismo para fastEthernet 0/2 en los core

CORE-2(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

Configurar interfaces troncales en los SW Core

CORE-1>ena
CORE-1#
CORE-1#conf ter
Enter configuration commands, one per line.  End with CNTL/Z.
CORE-1(config)#
CORE-1(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
CORE-1(config-if)#switchport trunk encapsulation dot1q
CORE-1(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

CORE-1(config-if)#
%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/1, changed state to down

%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/1, changed state to up

CORE-1(config-if)#exit
CORE-1(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/2
CORE-1(config-if)#switchport trunk encapsulation dot1q
CORE-1(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

CORE-1(config-if)#
%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/2, changed state to down

%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/2, changed state to up

CORE-1(config-if)#exit

______________________________________

CORE-2>ena
CORE-2#
CORE-2#conf ter
Enter configuration commands, one per line.  End with CNTL/Z.

CORE-2(config-if)#
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport trunk encapsulation dot1q
CORE-2(config-if)#
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

CORE-2(config-if)#
%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/1, changed state to down

%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/1, changed state to up

CORE-2(config-if)#exit
CORE-2(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/2
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport trunk encapsulation dot1q
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport mode trunk



_____________________________________

Configurar interfaces troncales en los SW Core

SW-Piso1(config)#interface gigabitEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso1(config-if)#switchport mode trunk


SW-Piso2(config)#interface gigabitEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso2(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

SW-Piso3(config)#interface gigabitEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso3(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

Conectar equipos a su respectiva VLAN

SW-Piso1(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso1(config-if)#switchport access vlan 52


SW-Piso2(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso2(config-if)#switchport access vlan 53


SW-Piso3(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso3(config-if)#switchport access vlan 53


SW-Piso4(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso4(config-if)#switchport access vlan 52
_____________________________________

Configurar los Gateway en los SW Core

CORE-1(config)#interface vlan 52
CORE-1(config-if)#ip address 192.168.117.1 255.255.255.0
CORE-1(config-if)#exit
CORE-1(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-1(config-if)#ip address 172.16.117.1 255.255.255.0



CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 52
CORE-2(config-if)#ip address 192.168.117.1 255.255.255.0
CORE-2(config-if)#
CORE-2(config-if)#exit
CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-2(config-if)#ip address 172.16.117.1 255.255.255.0

___________________________________

Finalmente, configurar el respectivo Gateway en los pc según la vlan a la que pertenece

___________________________________

Dar prioridad

CORE-1(config)#interface vlan 52
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 52 ip 192.168.117.1
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 52 priority 110


CORE-1(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 53 ip 172.16.117.1
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 53 priority 110

_____________________________________

CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 52
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 52 ip 192.168.117.1
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 52 priority 100

CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 53 ip 172.16.117.1
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 53 priority 100


_____________________________________

interface gigabitethernet0/1
switchport mode trunk
switchport trunk allowed vlan 52,53

Hacer lo mismo para fastEthernet 0/2 en los core

CORE-2(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport mode trunk
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport trunk allowed vlan 52,53

SW-Piso1(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso1(config-if)#switchport mode Access


SW-Piso2(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso2(config-if)#switchport mode Access


SW-Piso3(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso3(config-if)#switchport mode access


SW-Piso4(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso4(config-if)#switchport mode Access

______________________________________

Configurar interfaces troncales en los SW Core

CORE-1>ena
CORE-1#
CORE-1#conf ter
Enter configuration commands, one per line.  End with CNTL/Z.
CORE-1(config)#
CORE-1(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
CORE-1(config-if)#switchport trunk encapsulation dot1q
CORE-1(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

CORE-1(config-if)#
%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/1, changed state to down

%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/1, changed state to up

CORE-1(config-if)#exit
CORE-1(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/2
CORE-1(config-if)#switchport trunk encapsulation dot1q
CORE-1(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

CORE-1(config-if)#
%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/2, changed state to down

%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/2, changed state to up

CORE-1(config-if)#exit

______________________________________

CORE-2>ena
CORE-2#
CORE-2#conf ter
Enter configuration commands, one per line.  End with CNTL/Z.
CORE-2(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
CORE-2(config-if)#
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport trunk encapsulation dot1q
CORE-2(config-if)#
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

CORE-2(config-if)#
%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/1, changed state to down

%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/1, changed state to up

CORE-2(config-if)#exit
CORE-2(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/2
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport trunk encapsulation dot1q
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport mode trunk



_____________________________________

Configurar interfaces troncales en los SW Core

SW-Piso1(config)#interface gigabitEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso1(config-if)#switchport mode trunk


SW-Piso2(config)#interface gigabitEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso2(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

SW-Piso3(config)#interface gigabitEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso3(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

__________________________________

Conectar equipos a su respectiva VLAN

SW-Piso1(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso1(config-if)#switchport access vlan 52


SW-Piso2(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso2(config-if)#switchport access vlan 53


SW-Piso3(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso3(config-if)#switchport access vlan 53


SW-Piso4(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso4(config-if)#switchport access vlan 52
_____________________________________

Configurar los Gateway en los SW Core

CORE-1(config)#interface vlan 52
CORE-1(config-if)#ip address 192.168.117.1 255.255.255.0
CORE-1(config-if)#exit
CORE-1(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-1(config-if)#ip address 172.16.117.1 255.255.255.0



CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 52
CORE-2(config-if)#ip address 192.168.117.1 255.255.255.0
CORE-2(config-if)#
CORE-2(config-if)#exit
CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-2(config-if)#ip address 172.16.117.1 255.255.255.0

___________________________________

Finalmente, configurar el respectivo Gateway en los pc según la vlan a la que pertenece

___________________________________

Dar prioridad

CORE-1(config)#interface vlan 52
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 52 ip 192.168.117.1
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 52 priority 110


CORE-1(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 53 ip 172.16.117.1
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 53 priority 110

_____________________________________

CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 52
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 52 ip 192.168.117.1
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 52 priority 100

CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 53 ip 172.16.117.1
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 53 priority 100


_____________________________________

interface gigabitethernet0/1
switchport mode trunk
switchport trunk allowed vlan 52,53

Hacer lo mismo para fastEthernet 0/2 en los core

CORE-2(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport mode trunk
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport trunk allowed vlan 52,53

CORE-1(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 53 ip 172.16.117.1
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 53 priority 110

_____________________________________

CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 52
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 52 ip 192.168.117.1
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 52 priority 100

CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 53 ip 172.16.117.1
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 53 priority 100


_____________________________________

interface gigabitethernet0/1
switchport mode trunk
switchport trunk allowed vlan 52,53

Hacer lo mismo para fastEthernet 0/2 en los core

CORE-2(config-if)#switchport mode trunk
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport trunk allowed vlan 52,53

Configurar interfaces troncales en los SW Core

CORE-1>ena
CORE-1#
CORE-1#conf ter
Enter configuration commands, one per line.  End with CNTL/Z.
CORE-1(config)#

CORE-1(config-if)#switchport trunk encapsulation dot1q
CORE-1(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

CORE-1(config-if)#
%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/1, changed state to down

%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/1, changed state to up

CORE-1(config-if)#exit
CORE-1(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/2
CORE-1(config-if)#switchport trunk encapsulation dot1q
CORE-1(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

CORE-1(config-if)#
%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/2, changed state to down

%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/2, changed state to up

CORE-1(config-if)#exit

Configurar interfaces troncales en los SW Core

CORE-1>ena
CORE-1#
CORE-1#conf ter
Enter configuration commands, one per line.  End with CNTL/Z.
CORE-1(config)#
CORE-1(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
CORE-1(config-if)#switchport trunk encapsulation dot1q
CORE-1(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

CORE-1(config-if)#
%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/1, changed state to down

%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/1, changed state to up

CORE-1(config-if)#exit
CORE-1(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/2
CORE-1(config-if)#switchport trunk encapsulation dot1q
CORE-1(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

CORE-1(config-if)#
%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/2, changed state to down

%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/2, changed state to up

CORE-1(config-if)#exit

______________________________________

CORE-2>ena

CORE-2(config-if)#
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

CORE-2(config-if)#
%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/1, changed state to down

%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/1, changed state to up

CORE-2(config-if)#exit
CORE-2(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/2

CORE-2(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

Configurar los Gateway en los SW Core

CORE-1(config-if)#exit
CORE-1(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-1(config-if)#ip address 172.16.117.1 255.255.255.0



CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 52
CORE-2(config-if)#ip address 192.168.117.1 255.255.255.0
CORE-2(config-if)#
CORE-2(config-if)#exit
CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-2(config-if)#ip address 172.16.117.1 255.255.255.0

___________________________________

Finalmente, configurar el respectivo Gateway en los pc según la vlan a la que pertenece

___________________________________

Dar prioridad

CORE-1(config)#interface vlan 52
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 52 ip 192.168.117.1
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 52 priority 110


CORE-1(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 53 ip 172.16.117.1
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 53 priority 110

_____________________________________

CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 52
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 52 ip 192.168.117.1
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 52 priority 100

CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 53 ip 172.16.117.1
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 53 priority 100


_____________________________________

interface gigabitethernet0/1
switchport mode trunk
switchport trunk allowed vlan 52,53

Hacer lo mismo para fastEthernet 0/2 en los core

CORE-2(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport mode trunk
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport trunk allowed vlan 52,53

CORE-2(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
CORE-2(config-if)#
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport trunk encapsulation dot1q
CORE-2(config-if)#
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

CORE-2(config-if)#
%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/1, changed state to down

%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/1, changed state to up

CORE-2(config-if)#exit
CORE-2(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/2
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport trunk encapsulation dot1q
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport mode trunk



_____________________________________

Configurar interfaces troncales en los SW Core

SW-Piso1(config)#interface gigabitEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso1(config-if)#switchport mode trunk


SW-Piso2(config)#interface gigabitEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso2(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

SW-Piso3(config)#interface gigabitEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso3(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

__________________________________

Conectar equipos a su respectiva VLAN

SW-Piso1(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso1(config-if)#switchport access vlan 52


SW-Piso2(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso2(config-if)#switchport access vlan 53


SW-Piso3(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso3(config-if)#switchport access vlan 53


SW-Piso4(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso4(config-if)#switchport access vlan 52
_____________________________________

Configurar los Gateway en los SW Core

CORE-1(config)#interface vlan 52
CORE-1(config-if)#ip address 192.168.117.1 255.255.255.0
CORE-1(config-if)#exit
CORE-1(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-1(config-if)#ip address 172.16.117.1 255.255.255.0



CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 52
CORE-2(config-if)#ip address 192.168.117.1 255.255.255.0
CORE-2(config-if)#
CORE-2(config-if)#exit
CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-2(config-if)#ip address 172.16.117.1 255.255.255.0

___________________________________

Finalmente, configurar el respectivo Gateway en los pc según la vlan a la que pertenece

___________________________________

Dar prioridad

CORE-1(config)#interface vlan 52
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 52 ip 192.168.117.1
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 52 priority 110


CORE-1(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 53 ip 172.16.117.1
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 53 priority 110

_____________________________________

CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 52
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 52 ip 192.168.117.1
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 52 priority 100

CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 53 ip 172.16.117.1
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 53 priority 100


_____________________________________

interface gigabitethernet0/1
switchport mode trunk
switchport trunk allowed vlan 52,53

Hacer lo mismo para fastEthernet 0/2 en los core

CORE-2(config-if)#switchport mode trunk
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport trunk allowed vlan 52,53

Configurar interfaces de acceso en los switches

SW-Piso1(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso1(config-if)#switchport mode Access


SW-Piso2(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso2(config-if)#switchport mode Access


SW-Piso3(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso3(config-if)#switchport mode access


SW-Piso4(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso4(config-if)#switchport mode Access

______________________________________

Configurar interfaces troncales en los SW Core

CORE-1>ena
CORE-1#
CORE-1#conf ter
Enter configuration commands, one per line.  End with CNTL/Z.
CORE-1(config)#
CORE-1(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
CORE-1(config-if)#switchport trunk encapsulation dot1q
CORE-1(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

CORE-1(config-if)#
%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/1, changed state to down

%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/1, changed state to up

CORE-1(config-if)#exit
CORE-1(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/2
CORE-1(config-if)#switchport trunk encapsulation dot1q
CORE-1(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

CORE-1(config-if)#
%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/2, changed state to down

%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/2, changed state to up

CORE-1(config-if)#exit

______________________________________

CORE-2>ena
CORE-2#
CORE-2#conf ter
Enter configuration commands, one per line.  End with CNTL/Z.
CORE-2(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
CORE-2(config-if)#
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport trunk encapsulation dot1q
CORE-2(config-if)#
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

CORE-2(config-if)#
%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/1, changed state to down

%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/1, changed state to up

CORE-2(config-if)#exit
CORE-2(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/2
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport trunk encapsulation dot1q
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport mode trunk



_____________________________________

Configurar interfaces troncales en los SW Core

SW-Piso1(config)#interface gigabitEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso1(config-if)#switchport mode trunk


SW-Piso2(config)#interface gigabitEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso2(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

SW-Piso3(config)#interface gigabitEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso3(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

__________________________________

Conectar equipos a su respectiva VLAN

SW-Piso1(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso1(config-if)#switchport access vlan 52


SW-Piso2(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso2(config-if)#switchport access vlan 53


SW-Piso3(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso3(config-if)#switchport access vlan 53


SW-Piso4(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso4(config-if)#switchport access vlan 52
_____________________________________

Configurar los Gateway en los SW Core

CORE-1(config)#interface vlan 52
CORE-1(config-if)#ip address 192.168.117.1 255.255.255.0
CORE-1(config-if)#exit
CORE-1(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-1(config-if)#ip address 172.16.117.1 255.255.255.0



CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 52
CORE-2(config-if)#ip address 192.168.117.1 255.255.255.0
CORE-2(config-if)#
CORE-2(config-if)#exit
CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-2(config-if)#ip address 172.16.117.1 255.255.255.0

___________________________________

Finalmente, configurar el respectivo Gateway en los pc según la vlan a la que pertenece

___________________________________

Dar prioridad

CORE-1(config)#interface vlan 52
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 52 ip 192.168.117.1
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 52 priority 110


CORE-1(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 53 ip 172.16.117.1
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 53 priority 110

_____________________________________

CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 52
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 52 ip 192.168.117.1
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 52 priority 100

CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 53 ip 172.16.117.1
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 53 priority 100


_____________________________________

interface gigabitethernet0/1
switchport mode trunk
switchport trunk allowed vlan 52,53

Hacer lo mismo para fastEthernet 0/2 en los core

CORE-2(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport mode trunk
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport trunk allowed vlan 52,53

CORE-1>ena
CORE-1#

CORE-1(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

CORE-1(config-if)#switchport trunk encapsulation dot1q
CORE-1(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

CORE-1(config-if)#
%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/2, changed state to down

Configurar los Gateway en los SW Core

CORE-1(config)#interface vlan 52

CORE-1(config-if)#exit
CORE-1(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-1(config-if)#ip address 172.16.117.1 255.255.255.0



CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 52
CORE-2(config-if)#ip address 192.168.117.1 255.255.255.0
CORE-2(config-if)#
CORE-2(config-if)#exit
CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-2(config-if)#ip address 172.16.117.1 255.255.255.0

___________________________________

Finalmente, configurar el respectivo Gateway en los pc según la vlan a la que pertenece

___________________________________

Dar prioridad

CORE-1(config)#interface vlan 52
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 52 ip 192.168.117.1
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 52 priority 110


CORE-1(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 53 ip 172.16.117.1
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 53 priority 110

_____________________________________

CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 52
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 52 ip 192.168.117.1
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 52 priority 100

CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 53 ip 172.16.117.1
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 53 priority 100


_____________________________________

interface gigabitethernet0/1
switchport mode trunk
switchport trunk allowed vlan 52,53

Hacer lo mismo para fastEthernet 0/2 en los core

CORE-2(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport mode trunk
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport trunk allowed vlan 52,53

CORE-1(config-if)#ip address 192.168.117.1 255.255.255.0
CORE-1(config-if)#exit
CORE-1(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-1(config-if)#ip address 172.16.117.1 255.255.255.0



CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 52
CORE-2(config-if)#ip address 192.168.117.1 255.255.255.0
CORE-2(config-if)#
CORE-2(config-if)#exit
CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-2(config-if)#ip address 172.16.117.1 255.255.255.0

___________________________________

Finalmente, configurar el respectivo Gateway en los pc según la vlan a la que pertenece

___________________________________

Dar prioridad

CORE-1(config)#interface vlan 52
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 52 ip 192.168.117.1
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 52 priority 110


CORE-1(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 53 ip 172.16.117.1
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 53 priority 110

_____________________________________

CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 52
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 52 ip 192.168.117.1
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 52 priority 100

CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 53 ip 172.16.117.1
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 53 priority 100


_____________________________________

interface gigabitethernet0/1
switchport mode trunk
switchport trunk allowed vlan 52,53

Hacer lo mismo para fastEthernet 0/2 en los core

CORE-2(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport mode trunk
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport trunk allowed vlan 52,53

Configurar interfaces troncales en los SW Core

CORE-1>ena
CORE-1#
CORE-1#conf ter
Enter configuration commands, one per line.  End with CNTL/Z.
CORE-1(config)#
CORE-1(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
CORE-1(config-if)#switchport trunk encapsulation dot1q
CORE-1(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

CORE-1(config-if)#
%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/1, changed state to down

%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/1, changed state to up

CORE-1(config-if)#exit
CORE-1(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/2
CORE-1(config-if)#switchport trunk encapsulation dot1q
CORE-1(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

CORE-1(config-if)#
%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/2, changed state to down

%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/2, changed state to up

CORE-1(config-if)#exit

______________________________________

CORE-2>ena
CORE-2#
CORE-2#conf ter

CORE-2(config-if)#
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

CORE-2(config-if)#
%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/1, changed state to down

%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/1, changed state to up

CORE-2(config-if)#exit
CORE-2(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/2
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport trunk encapsulation dot1q
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

Configurar interfaces troncales en los SW Core

CORE-1>ena
CORE-1#
CORE-1#conf ter
Enter configuration commands, one per line.  End with CNTL/Z.
CORE-1(config)#
CORE-1(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
CORE-1(config-if)#switchport trunk encapsulation dot1q
CORE-1(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

CORE-1(config-if)#
%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/1, changed state to down

%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/1, changed state to up

CORE-1(config-if)#exit
CORE-1(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/2
CORE-1(config-if)#switchport trunk encapsulation dot1q
CORE-1(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

CORE-1(config-if)#
%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/2, changed state to down

%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/2, changed state to up

CORE-1(config-if)#exit

______________________________________

CORE-2>ena
CORE-2#
CORE-2#conf ter
Enter configuration commands, one per line.  End with CNTL/Z.
CORE-2(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
CORE-2(config-if)#
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport trunk encapsulation dot1q
CORE-2(config-if)#
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

CORE-2(config-if)#
%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/1, changed state to down

%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/1, changed state to up

CORE-2(config-if)#exit
CORE-2(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/2
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport trunk encapsulation dot1q
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport mode trunk



_____________________________________

Configurar interfaces troncales en los SW Core

SW-Piso1(config)#interface gigabitEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso1(config-if)#switchport mode trunk


SW-Piso2(config)#interface gigabitEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso2(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

SW-Piso3(config)#interface gigabitEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso3(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

__________________________________

Conectar equipos a su respectiva VLAN

SW-Piso1(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso1(config-if)#switchport access vlan 52


SW-Piso2(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso2(config-if)#switchport access vlan 53


SW-Piso3(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso3(config-if)#switchport access vlan 53


SW-Piso4(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso4(config-if)#switchport access vlan 52
_____________________________________

Configurar los Gateway en los SW Core

CORE-1(config)#interface vlan 52
CORE-1(config-if)#ip address 192.168.117.1 255.255.255.0
CORE-1(config-if)#exit
CORE-1(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-1(config-if)#ip address 172.16.117.1 255.255.255.0



CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 52
CORE-2(config-if)#ip address 192.168.117.1 255.255.255.0
CORE-2(config-if)#
CORE-2(config-if)#exit
CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-2(config-if)#ip address 172.16.117.1 255.255.255.0

___________________________________

Finalmente, configurar el respectivo Gateway en los pc según la vlan a la que pertenece

___________________________________

Dar prioridad

CORE-1(config)#interface vlan 52
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 52 ip 192.168.117.1
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 52 priority 110


CORE-1(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 53 ip 172.16.117.1
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 53 priority 110

_____________________________________

CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 52
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 52 ip 192.168.117.1
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 52 priority 100

CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 53 ip 172.16.117.1
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 53 priority 100


_____________________________________

interface gigabitethernet0/1
switchport mode trunk
switchport trunk allowed vlan 52,53

Hacer lo mismo para fastEthernet 0/2 en los core

CORE-2(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport mode trunk
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport trunk allowed vlan 52,53

CORE-1(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

CORE-1(config-if)#
%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/1, changed state to down

%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/1, changed state to up

CORE-1(config-if)#exit
CORE-1(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/2
CORE-1(config-if)#switchport trunk encapsulation dot1q
CORE-1(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

CORE-1(config-if)#
%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/2, changed state to down

%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/2, changed state to up

CORE-1(config-if)#exit

Configurar los Gateway en los SW Core

CORE-2(config-if)#

CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 53

Finalmente, configurar el respectivo Gateway en los pc según la vlan a la que pertenece

___________________________________

CORE-1(config-if)#standby 52 priority 110


CORE-1(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 53 ip 172.16.117.1
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 53 priority 110

_____________________________________

CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 52
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 52 ip 192.168.117.1
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 52 priority 100

CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 53 ip 172.16.117.1
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 53 priority 100

interface gigabitethernet0/1
switchport mode trunk
switchport trunk allowed vlan 52,53

Configurar interfaces troncales en los SW Core

CORE-1>ena
CORE-1#
CORE-1#conf ter
Enter configuration commands, one per line.  End with CNTL/Z.
CORE-1(config)#
CORE-1(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
CORE-1(config-if)#switchport trunk encapsulation dot1q
CORE-1(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

CORE-1(config-if)#
%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/1, changed state to down

%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/1, changed state to up

CORE-1(config-if)#exit
CORE-1(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/2
CORE-1(config-if)#switchport trunk encapsulation dot1q
CORE-1(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

CORE-1(config-if)#
%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/2, changed state to down

%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/2, changed state to up

CORE-1(config-if)#exit

CORE-2(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

Configurar interfaces troncales en los SW Core

CORE-1>ena
CORE-1#
CORE-1#conf ter
Enter configuration commands, one per line.  End with CNTL/Z.
CORE-1(config)#
CORE-1(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
CORE-1(config-if)#switchport trunk encapsulation dot1q
CORE-1(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

CORE-1(config-if)#
%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/1, changed state to down

%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/1, changed state to up

CORE-1(config-if)#exit
CORE-1(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/2
CORE-1(config-if)#switchport trunk encapsulation dot1q
CORE-1(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

CORE-1(config-if)#
%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/2, changed state to down

%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/2, changed state to up

CORE-1(config-if)#exit

______________________________________

CORE-2>ena
CORE-2#
CORE-2#conf ter
Enter configuration commands, one per line.  End with CNTL/Z.
CORE-2(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
CORE-2(config-if)#
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport trunk encapsulation dot1q
CORE-2(config-if)#
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

CORE-2(config-if)#
%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/1, changed state to down

%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/1, changed state to up

CORE-2(config-if)#exit
CORE-2(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/2
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport trunk encapsulation dot1q
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport mode trunk



_____________________________________

Configurar interfaces troncales en los SW Core

SW-Piso1(config-if)#switchport mode trunk


SW-Piso2(config)#interface gigabitEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso2(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

SW-Piso3(config)#interface gigabitEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso3(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

__________________________________

Conectar equipos a su respectiva VLAN

SW-Piso1(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso1(config-if)#switchport access vlan 52


SW-Piso2(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso2(config-if)#switchport access vlan 53


SW-Piso3(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso3(config-if)#switchport access vlan 53


SW-Piso4(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso4(config-if)#switchport access vlan 52
_____________________________________

Configurar los Gateway en los SW Core

CORE-1(config)#interface vlan 52
CORE-1(config-if)#ip address 192.168.117.1 255.255.255.0
CORE-1(config-if)#exit
CORE-1(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-1(config-if)#ip address 172.16.117.1 255.255.255.0



CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 52
CORE-2(config-if)#ip address 192.168.117.1 255.255.255.0
CORE-2(config-if)#
CORE-2(config-if)#exit
CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-2(config-if)#ip address 172.16.117.1 255.255.255.0

___________________________________

Finalmente, configurar el respectivo Gateway en los pc según la vlan a la que pertenece

___________________________________

Dar prioridad

CORE-1(config)#interface vlan 52
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 52 ip 192.168.117.1
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 52 priority 110


CORE-1(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 53 ip 172.16.117.1
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 53 priority 110

_____________________________________

CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 52
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 52 ip 192.168.117.1
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 52 priority 100

CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 53 ip 172.16.117.1
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 53 priority 100


_____________________________________

interface gigabitethernet0/1
switchport mode trunk
switchport trunk allowed vlan 52,53

Hacer lo mismo para fastEthernet 0/2 en los core

CORE-2(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport mode trunk
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport trunk allowed vlan 52,53

CORE-1(config-if)#standby 53 priority 110

CORE-2(config-if)#standby 52 priority 100

CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 53 ip 172.16.117.1
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 53 priority 100

switchport trunk allowed vlan 52,53

Configurar interfaces de acceso en los switches

SW-Piso1(config-if)#switchport mode Access


SW-Piso2(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso2(config-if)#switchport mode Access


SW-Piso3(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso3(config-if)#switchport mode access


SW-Piso4(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso4(config-if)#switchport mode Access

______________________________________

Configurar interfaces troncales en los SW Core

CORE-1>ena
CORE-1#
CORE-1#conf ter
Enter configuration commands, one per line.  End with CNTL/Z.
CORE-1(config)#
CORE-1(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
CORE-1(config-if)#switchport trunk encapsulation dot1q
CORE-1(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

CORE-1(config-if)#
%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/1, changed state to down

%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/1, changed state to up

CORE-1(config-if)#exit
CORE-1(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/2
CORE-1(config-if)#switchport trunk encapsulation dot1q
CORE-1(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

CORE-1(config-if)#
%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/2, changed state to down

%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/2, changed state to up

CORE-1(config-if)#exit

______________________________________

CORE-2>ena
CORE-2#
CORE-2#conf ter
Enter configuration commands, one per line.  End with CNTL/Z.
CORE-2(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
CORE-2(config-if)#
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport trunk encapsulation dot1q
CORE-2(config-if)#
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

CORE-2(config-if)#
%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/1, changed state to down

%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/1, changed state to up

CORE-2(config-if)#exit
CORE-2(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/2
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport trunk encapsulation dot1q
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport mode trunk



_____________________________________

Configurar interfaces troncales en los SW Core

SW-Piso1(config)#interface gigabitEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso1(config-if)#switchport mode trunk


SW-Piso2(config)#interface gigabitEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso2(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

SW-Piso3(config)#interface gigabitEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso3(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

__________________________________

Conectar equipos a su respectiva VLAN

SW-Piso1(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso1(config-if)#switchport access vlan 52


SW-Piso2(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso2(config-if)#switchport access vlan 53


SW-Piso3(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso3(config-if)#switchport access vlan 53


SW-Piso4(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso4(config-if)#switchport access vlan 52
_____________________________________

Configurar los Gateway en los SW Core

CORE-1(config)#interface vlan 52
CORE-1(config-if)#ip address 192.168.117.1 255.255.255.0
CORE-1(config-if)#exit
CORE-1(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-1(config-if)#ip address 172.16.117.1 255.255.255.0



CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 52
CORE-2(config-if)#ip address 192.168.117.1 255.255.255.0
CORE-2(config-if)#
CORE-2(config-if)#exit
CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-2(config-if)#ip address 172.16.117.1 255.255.255.0

___________________________________

Finalmente, configurar el respectivo Gateway en los pc según la vlan a la que pertenece

___________________________________

Dar prioridad

CORE-1(config)#interface vlan 52
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 52 ip 192.168.117.1
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 52 priority 110


CORE-1(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 53 ip 172.16.117.1
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 53 priority 110

_____________________________________

CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 52
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 52 ip 192.168.117.1
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 52 priority 100

CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 53 ip 172.16.117.1
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 53 priority 100


_____________________________________

interface gigabitethernet0/1
switchport mode trunk
switchport trunk allowed vlan 52,53

Hacer lo mismo para fastEthernet 0/2 en los core

CORE-2(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport mode trunk
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport trunk allowed vlan 52,53

CORE-1(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

Finalmente, configurar el respectivo Gateway en los pc según la vlan a la que pertenece

Dar prioridad

CORE-1(config-if)#standby 52 priority 110

CORE-1(config-if)#standby 53 ip 172.16.117.1
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 53 priority 110

_____________________________________

CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 52
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 52 ip 192.168.117.1
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 52 priority 100

CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 53 ip 172.16.117.1
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 53 priority 100


_____________________________________

interface gigabitethernet0/1
switchport mode trunk
switchport trunk allowed vlan 52,53

Hacer lo mismo para fastEthernet 0/2 en los core

CORE-2(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport mode trunk
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport trunk allowed vlan 52,53

SW-Piso2(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

SW-Piso3(config)#interface gigabitEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso3(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

__________________________________

Conectar equipos a su respectiva VLAN

SW-Piso1(config-if)#switchport access vlan 52


SW-Piso2(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso2(config-if)#switchport access vlan 53


SW-Piso3(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso3(config-if)#switchport access vlan 53


SW-Piso4(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso4(config-if)#switchport access vlan 52
_____________________________________

Configurar los Gateway en los SW Core

CORE-1(config)#interface vlan 52
CORE-1(config-if)#ip address 192.168.117.1 255.255.255.0
CORE-1(config-if)#exit
CORE-1(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-1(config-if)#ip address 172.16.117.1 255.255.255.0



CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 52
CORE-2(config-if)#ip address 192.168.117.1 255.255.255.0
CORE-2(config-if)#
CORE-2(config-if)#exit
CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-2(config-if)#ip address 172.16.117.1 255.255.255.0

___________________________________

Finalmente, configurar el respectivo Gateway en los pc según la vlan a la que pertenece

___________________________________

Dar prioridad

CORE-1(config)#interface vlan 52
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 52 ip 192.168.117.1
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 52 priority 110


CORE-1(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 53 ip 172.16.117.1
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 53 priority 110

_____________________________________

CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 52
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 52 ip 192.168.117.1
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 52 priority 100

CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 53 ip 172.16.117.1
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 53 priority 100


_____________________________________

interface gigabitethernet0/1
switchport mode trunk
switchport trunk allowed vlan 52,53

CORE-2(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

CORE-1(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

CORE-1(config-if)#
%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/1, changed state to down

%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/1, changed state to up

CORE-1(config-if)#exit
CORE-1(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/2
CORE-1(config-if)#switchport trunk encapsulation dot1q
CORE-1(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

CORE-1(config-if)#
%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/2, changed state to down

%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/2, changed state to up

CORE-1(config-if)#exit

CORE-1>ena
CORE-1#
CORE-1#conf ter
Enter configuration commands, one per line.  End with CNTL/Z.
CORE-1(config)#
CORE-1(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
CORE-1(config-if)#switchport trunk encapsulation dot1q
CORE-1(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

CORE-1(config-if)#
%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/1, changed state to down

%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/1, changed state to up

CORE-1(config-if)#exit
CORE-1(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/2
CORE-1(config-if)#switchport trunk encapsulation dot1q
CORE-1(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

CORE-1(config-if)#
%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/2, changed state to down

%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/2, changed state to up

CORE-1(config-if)#exit

______________________________________

CORE-2>ena
CORE-2#
CORE-2#conf ter
Enter configuration commands, one per line.  End with CNTL/Z.

CORE-2(config-if)#
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport trunk encapsulation dot1q
CORE-2(config-if)#
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

CORE-2(config-if)#
%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/1, changed state to down

%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/1, changed state to up

CORE-2(config-if)#exit
CORE-2(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/2
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport trunk encapsulation dot1q
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport mode trunk



_____________________________________

Configurar interfaces troncales en los SW Core

SW-Piso1(config)#interface gigabitEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso1(config-if)#switchport mode trunk


SW-Piso2(config)#interface gigabitEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso2(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

SW-Piso3(config)#interface gigabitEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso3(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

__________________________________

Conectar equipos a su respectiva VLAN

SW-Piso1(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso1(config-if)#switchport access vlan 52


SW-Piso2(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso2(config-if)#switchport access vlan 53


SW-Piso3(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso3(config-if)#switchport access vlan 53


SW-Piso4(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
SW-Piso4(config-if)#switchport access vlan 52
_____________________________________

Configurar los Gateway en los SW Core

CORE-1(config)#interface vlan 52
CORE-1(config-if)#ip address 192.168.117.1 255.255.255.0
CORE-1(config-if)#exit
CORE-1(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-1(config-if)#ip address 172.16.117.1 255.255.255.0



CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 52
CORE-2(config-if)#ip address 192.168.117.1 255.255.255.0
CORE-2(config-if)#
CORE-2(config-if)#exit
CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-2(config-if)#ip address 172.16.117.1 255.255.255.0

___________________________________

Finalmente, configurar el respectivo Gateway en los pc según la vlan a la que pertenece

___________________________________

Dar prioridad

CORE-1(config)#interface vlan 52
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 52 ip 192.168.117.1
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 52 priority 110


CORE-1(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 53 ip 172.16.117.1
CORE-1(config-if)#standby 53 priority 110

_____________________________________

CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 52
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 52 ip 192.168.117.1
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 52 priority 100

CORE-2(config)#interface vlan 53
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 53 ip 172.16.117.1
CORE-2(config-if)#standby 53 priority 100


_____________________________________

interface gigabitethernet0/1
switchport mode trunk
switchport trunk allowed vlan 52,53

Hacer lo mismo para fastEthernet 0/2 en los core

CORE-2(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport mode trunk
CORE-2(config-if)#switchport trunk allowed vlan 52,53

Configurar interfaces troncales en los SW Core

CORE-1>ena
CORE-1#
CORE-1#conf ter
Enter configuration commands, one per line.  End with CNTL/Z.
CORE-1(config)#
CORE-1(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/1
CORE-1(config-if)#switchport trunk encapsulation dot1q
CORE-1(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

CORE-1(config-if)#
%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/1, changed state to down

%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/1, changed state to up

CORE-1(config-if)#exit
CORE-1(config)#interface fastEthernet 0/2
CORE-1(config-if)#switchport trunk encapsulation dot1q
CORE-1(config-if)#switchport mode trunk

CORE-1(config-if)#
%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/2, changed state to down

%LINEPROTO-5-UPDOWN: Line protocol on Interface FastEthernet0/2, changed state to up

CORE-1(config-if)#exit
